     1	/* Data layer: JSON fetch wrapper (structured-error passthrough), the WS client,
     2	 * and the invalidation bus (SPEC §9). No client-side state store — the bus holds
     3	 * only a cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff delay, and one flush callback; every
     4	 * flush makes the active screen refetch. WS batches are an invalidation signal:
     5	 * onmessage reads only msg.cursor, never the events array.
     6	 * Classic script: attaches Planner.api and Planner.bus; defines window.__plannerDebug. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	
    12	  // The sanctioned console hook: counters only, one console.debug per flush / open.
    13	  window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };
    14	
    15	  function makeError(code, message, extra) {
    16	    var err = new Error(message);
    17	    err.code = code;
    18	    if (extra) {
    19	      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
    20	        err[key] = extra[key];
    21	      });
    22	    }
    23	    return err;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  function makePlannerError(envelope, status) {
    27	    var err = new Error(envelope.message);
    28	    err.code = envelope.code;
    29	    err.detail = envelope.detail;
    30	    err.status = status;
    31	    err.isPlannerError = true;
    32	    return err;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  // --- fetchJson ---------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	  function fetchJson(path, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    var init = { method: options.method || "GET", headers: {} };
    40	    if (options.body !== undefined && options.body !== null) {
    41	      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    42	      init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    43	    }
    44	    return fetch(path, init).then(
    45	      function (response) {
    46	        return response.text().then(function (raw) {
    47	          return { response: response, raw: raw };
    48	        });
    49	      },
    50	      function () {
    51	        return Promise.reject(makeError("network", "network error"));
    52	      }
    53	    ).then(function (pair) {
    54	      var response = pair.response;
    55	      var raw = pair.raw;
    56	      if (response.ok) {
    57	        try {
    58	          return raw === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(raw);
    59	        } catch (parseErr) {
    60	          return Promise.reject(
    61	            makeError("bad_json", "invalid JSON in response", { status: response.status })
    62	          );
    63	        }
    64	      }
    65	      var parsed = null;
    66	      try {
    67	        parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    68	      } catch (parseErr) {
    69	        parsed = null;
    70	      }
    71	      if (parsed && parsed.error && parsed.error.code) {
    72	        return Promise.reject(makePlannerError(parsed.error, response.status));
    73	      }
    74	      return Promise.reject(
    75	        makeError("http_error", "HTTP " + response.status, { status: response.status })
    76	      );
    77	    });
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  Planner.api = { fetchJson: fetchJson };
    81	
    82	  // --- the invalidation bus ----------------------------------------------------
    83	
    84	  var cursor = 0;
    85	  var socket = null;
    86	  var debounceMs = config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    87	  var flushTimer = null;
    88	  var retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
    89	  var onFlush = null;
    90	  var started = false;
    91	
    92	  function flush() {
    93	    flushTimer = null;
    94	    window.__plannerDebug.flushes += 1;
    95	    console.debug("[planner] flush " + window.__plannerDebug.flushes);
    96	    if (onFlush) {
    97	      onFlush();
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function schedule() {
   102	    clearTimeout(flushTimer);
   103	    flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  function connect() {
   107	    var scheme = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
   108	    var url = scheme + location.host + config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor;
   109	    socket = new WebSocket(url);
   110	    socket.onopen = function () {
   111	      retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
   112	      window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1;
   113	      console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor);
   114	    };
   115	    socket.onmessage = function (event) {
   116	      var msg;
   117	      try {
   118	        msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
   119	      } catch (parseErr) {
   120	        console.debug("[planner] ws bad json");
   121	        return;
   122	      }
   123	      if (typeof msg.cursor === "number") {
   124	        cursor = msg.cursor;
   125	        window.__plannerDebug.cursor = cursor;
   126	      }
   127	      schedule();
   128	    };
   129	    socket.onclose = function () {
   130	      socket = null;
   131	      setTimeout(connect, retryMs);
   132	      retryMs = Math.min(retryMs * config.WS_RETRY_FACTOR, config.WS_RETRY_MAX_MS);
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  Planner.bus = {
   137	    onInvalidate: function (fn) {
   138	      onFlush = fn;
   139	    },
   140	    start: function (ms) {
   141	      if (started) {
   142	        return;
   143	      }
   144	      started = true;
   145	      debounceMs = ms;
   146	      connect();
   147	    }
   148	  };
   149	})();

     1	/* Minimal safe markdown renderer. The ONLY innerHTML assignment in the whole
     2	 * codebase lives here — and it receives a string whose every character of user
     3	 * origin was HTML-escaped BEFORE any parsing, so no un-entified "<", ">", or quote
     4	 * can exist. Every tag is renderer-authored from a closed set; the only attribute
     5	 * emitted is a scheme-whitelisted href. Classic script: attaches Planner.markdown. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	
    10	  // Code-span placeholder sentinel (NUL). Stripped from raw input first, so a
    11	  // user string can never forge a placeholder.
    12	  var NUL = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	  var RESTORE_RE = new RegExp(NUL + "(\\d+)" + NUL, "g");
    14	
    15	  function escapeHtml(s) {
    16	    return s
    17	      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    18	      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    19	      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    20	      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    21	      .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  var SCHEME_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/;
    25	
    26	  function safeHref(url) {
    27	    // Validate a normalized copy: browsers strip leading C0 controls/spaces
    28	    // (and interior tab/newline) when parsing URLs, so "\u0001javascript:..."
    29	    // would read as scheme-less here yet execute after normalization. Our strip
    30	    // is a superset of the browser's, so anything passing this check cannot
    31	    // normalize to a non-whitelisted scheme.
    32	    var norm = url.replace(/[\u0000-\u0020]+/g, "").toLowerCase();
    33	    if (SCHEME_RE.test(norm)) {
    34	      return (
    35	        norm.indexOf("http:") === 0 ||
    36	        norm.indexOf("https:") === 0 ||
    37	        norm.indexOf("mailto:") === 0
    38	      );
    39	    }
    40	    return true; // scheme-less (relative, "#/ticket/...", "?", "/") is allowed
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function renderInline(line) {
    44	    var codes = [];
    45	    // 1. Code spans first — protected from all further processing.
    46	    line = line.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function (match, inner) {
    47	      var index = codes.length;
    48	      codes.push(inner);
    49	      return NUL + index + NUL;
    50	    });
    51	    // 2. Links — href is scheme-whitelisted; the value is already escaped.
    52	    line = line.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^()\s]+)\)/g, function (match, text, url) {
    53	      if (safeHref(url)) {
    54	        return '<a href="' + url + '">' + text + "</a>";
    55	      }
    56	      return match;
    57	    });
    58	    // 3. Bold before italic so "**" is not eaten by "*".
    59	    line = line.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>");
    60	    // 4. Italic.
    61	    line = line.replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    62	    line = line.replace(/_([^_]+)_/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    63	    // 5. Restore code spans.
    64	    line = line.replace(RESTORE_RE, function (match, index) {
    65	      return "<code>" + codes[Number(index)] + "</code>";
    66	    });
    67	    return line;
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  function render(text) {
    71	    // 1. Strip the placeholder sentinel so collisions are impossible.
    72	    text = String(text).split(NUL).join("");
    73	    // 2. Escape everything once, before any parsing.
    74	    var escaped = escapeHtml(text);
    75	    var lines = escaped.split("\n");
    76	    var out = [];
    77	    var para = null;
    78	    var listType = null;
    79	    var listItems = null;
    80	    var i = 0;
    81	
    82	    function closePara() {
    83	      if (para) {
    84	        out.push("<p>" + para.join("<br>") + "</p>");
    85	        para = null;
    86	      }
    87	    }
    88	    function closeList() {
    89	      if (listItems) {
    90	        out.push("<" + listType + ">" + listItems.join("") + "</" + listType + ">");
    91	        listItems = null;
    92	        listType = null;
    93	      }
    94	    }
    95	    function closeBlocks() {
    96	      closePara();
    97	      closeList();
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    while (i < lines.length) {
   101	      var line = lines[i];
   102	      // Fenced code block — verbatim, no inline processing (already escaped).
   103	      if (/^```/.test(line)) {
   104	        closeBlocks();
   105	        i += 1;
   106	        var codeLines = [];
   107	        while (i < lines.length && !/^```/.test(lines[i])) {
   108	          codeLines.push(lines[i]);
   109	          i += 1;
   110	        }
   111	        if (i < lines.length) {
   112	          i += 1; // consume the closing fence
   113	        }
   114	        out.push("<pre><code>" + codeLines.join("\n") + "</code></pre>");
   115	        continue;
   116	      }
   117	      // Blank line closes the open block.
   118	      if (/^\s*$/.test(line)) {
   119	        closeBlocks();
   120	        i += 1;
   121	        continue;
   122	      }
   123	      // Heading — caps at h3 (four or more "#" render as h3).
   124	      var heading = /^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   125	      if (heading) {
   126	        closeBlocks();
   127	        var level = heading[1].length;
   128	        if (level > 3) {
   129	          level = 3;
   130	        }
   131	        out.push("<h" + level + ">" + renderInline(heading[2]) + "</h" + level + ">");
   132	        i += 1;
   133	        continue;
   134	      }
   135	      // Unordered list item.
   136	      var unordered = /^[-*+]\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   137	      if (unordered) {
   138	        closePara();
   139	        if (listType && listType !== "ul") {
   140	          closeList();
   141	        }
   142	        if (!listItems) {
   143	          listType = "ul";
   144	          listItems = [];
   145	        }
   146	        listItems.push("<li>" + renderInline(unordered[1]) + "</li>");
   147	        i += 1;
   148	        continue;
   149	      }
   150	      // Ordered list item.
   151	      var ordered = /^\d{1,9}[.)]\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   152	      if (ordered) {
   153	        closePara();
   154	        if (listType && listType !== "ol") {
   155	          closeList();
   156	        }
   157	        if (!listItems) {
   158	          listType = "ol";
   159	          listItems = [];
   160	        }
   161	        listItems.push("<li>" + renderInline(ordered[1]) + "</li>");
   162	        i += 1;
   163	        continue;
   164	      }
   165	      // Paragraph line — consecutive lines join with <br>.
   166	      closeList();
   167	      if (!para) {
   168	        para = [];
   169	      }
   170	      para.push(renderInline(line));
   171	      i += 1;
   172	    }
   173	    closeBlocks();
   174	
   175	    var div = document.createElement("div");
   176	    div.className = "markdown";
   177	    div.innerHTML = out.join("");
   178	    return div;
   179	  }
   180	
   181	  Planner.markdown = { render: render };
   182	})();

    93	                _log.warning("planner.core.loops unavailable; running without background loops")
    94	            else:
    95	                loops = start(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
    96	        try:
    97	            yield
    98	        finally:
    99	            if loops is not None:
   100	                await loops.stop()

     1	"""Plan-tree transforms (§6.3) as pure functions ``PlanTree → (new_tree, effects)``,
     2	plus JSON serialization of the tree for the ``days.plan`` column and for event
     3	payloads that carry ``old_tree``. No side effects; the data layer persists the
     4	returned tree and applies the effects. Stdlib + planner contract modules only."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	from dataclasses import replace
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict
    11	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    12	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    13	    AddTicketToDay,
    14	    Effect,
    15	    EmitEvent,
    16	    ReplanChild,
    17	    ReplanRoot,
    18	)
    19	
    20	# A node address in the plan tree: the literal ``"root"`` or a child ``position``.
    21	# Mirrors the plan_node_accepted / plan_node_invalidated payload contract, whose
    22	# ``{node}`` value is ``"root" | <child position>``.
    23	NodeRef = str | int
    24	
    25	
    26	def tree_to_dict(tree: PlanTree) -> JsonDict:
    27	    """{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}, ...]}.
    28	
    29	    NodeStatus serialized as its str value; child order preserved.
    30	    """
    31	    return {
    32	        "root": {"focus": tree.root.focus, "status": tree.root.status.value},
    33	        "children": [
    34	            {
    35	                "ticket_id": child.ticket_id,
    36	                "note": child.note,
    37	                "status": child.status.value,
    38	                "position": child.position,
    39	            }
    40	            for child in tree.children
    41	        ],
    42	    }
    43	
    44	
    45	def tree_from_dict(data: JsonDict) -> PlanTree:
    46	    """Inverse of ``tree_to_dict``. status parsed via ``NodeStatus(...)``."""
    47	    root = data["root"]
    48	    children = [
    49	        PlanNode(
    50	            ticket_id=child["ticket_id"],
    51	            note=child["note"],
    52	            status=NodeStatus(child["status"]),
    53	            position=child["position"],
    54	        )
    55	        for child in data["children"]
    56	    ]
    57	    return PlanTree(
    58	        root=PlanRoot(focus=root["focus"], status=NodeStatus(root["status"])),
    59	        children=children,
    60	    )
    61	
    62	
    63	def as_proposed(tree: PlanTree) -> PlanTree:
    64	    """Return a copy with root and every child status=proposed. The boundary
    65	    stores the judgment tree as a PROPOSED plan (§6.2)."""
    66	    return PlanTree(
    67	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.proposed),
    68	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.proposed) for child in tree.children],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def accept_node(tree: PlanTree, ref: NodeRef) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
    73	    """§6.3: accepting a node sets it accepted; accepting root does NOT cascade.
    74	
    75	    A child with a ticket_id also yields an AddTicketToDay effect (appended at
    76	    end if absent). Effect order: EmitEvent(plan_node_accepted) then (for a child
    77	    with a ticket_id) AddTicketToDay.
    78	    """
    79	    if ref == "root":
    80	        new_tree = PlanTree(
    81	            root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
    82	            children=[replace(child) for child in tree.children],
    83	        )
    84	        return new_tree, [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": "root"})]
    85	
    86	    accepted: PlanNode | None = None
    87	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
    88	    for child in tree.children:
    89	        if child.position == ref:
    90	            accepted = replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted)
    91	            children.append(accepted)
    92	        else:
    93	            children.append(replace(child))
    94	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
    95	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": ref})]
    96	    if accepted is not None and accepted.ticket_id:
    97	        effects.append(AddTicketToDay(accepted.ticket_id))
    98	    return new_tree, effects
    99	
   100	
   101	def accept_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   102	    """§6.3 accept-all: root + every child accepted. Effects =
   103	    [EmitEvent(plan_accepted_all, {})] then AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id) for
   104	    each child with a ticket_id, in child position order. Idempotent add is the
   105	    data layer's job (a17)."""
   106	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   107	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
   108	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted) for child in tree.children],
   109	    )
   110	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_accepted_all, {})]
   111	    for child in tree.children:
   112	        if child.ticket_id:
   113	            effects.append(AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id))
   114	    return new_tree, effects
   115	
   116	
   117	def invalidate_root(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   118	    """§6.3: root + EVERY child → invalidated; request exactly one root replan."""
   119	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   120	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.invalidated),
   121	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated) for child in tree.children],
   122	    )
   123	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   124	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": "root"}),
   125	        ReplanRoot(),
   126	    ]
   127	    return new_tree, effects
   128	
   129	
   130	def invalidate_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   131	    """§6.3: ONLY the child at ``position`` → invalidated; root and every other
   132	    child keep their prior status. Requests exactly one child replan."""
   133	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
   134	    for child in tree.children:
   135	        if child.position == position:
   136	            children.append(replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated))
   137	        else:
   138	            children.append(replace(child))
   139	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
   140	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   141	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": position}),
   142	        ReplanChild(position),
   143	    ]
   144	    return new_tree, effects
   145	
   146	
   147	def reject_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[None, list[Effect]]:
   148	    """§6.3 reject-all: plan cleared. New tree is None (the data layer writes
   149	    days.plan = NULL). The old tree is preserved in the event payload."""
   150	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   151	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_rejected, {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(tree)})
   152	    ]
   153	    return None, effects

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)

    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )
    91	
    92	
    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:
    94	    plan = days_data.load_plan(conn, did)
    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})
    97	    return plan
    98	
    99	
   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})
   103	
   104	
   105	# --- day routes ----------------------------------------------------------------
   106	
   107	
   108	@router.get("/day/{date}")
   109	async def get_day(date: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   110	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   111	    return _day_view(conn, did, clk.now_unix())
   112	
   113	
   114	@router.patch("/day/{date}")
   115	async def patch_day(date: str, body: DayPatchBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   116	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   117	    now = clk.now_unix()
   118	    if body.brief is None and body.notes is None:
   119	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no day fields to update", {})
   120	    if body.brief is not None:
   121	        with txn(conn):
   122	            days_data.set_brief(conn, did, body.brief, now)
   123	    if body.notes is not None:
   124	        with txn(conn):
   125	            days_data.set_notes(conn, did, body.notes, now)
   126	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   127	
   128	
   129	@router.post("/day/{date}/tickets")
   130	async def add_day_ticket(date: str, body: AddDayTicketBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg,
   131	                         clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   132	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   133	    now = clk.now_unix()
   134	    read_ticket(conn, body.ticket_id)  # existence guard (avoids a raw FK 500)
   135	    with txn(conn):
   136	        days_data.add_day_ticket(conn, did, body.ticket_id, now)
   137	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   138	
   139	
   140	@router.delete("/day/{date}/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   141	async def remove_day_ticket(date: str, ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg,
   142	                            clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   143	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   144	    now = clk.now_unix()
   145	    with txn(conn):
   146	        days_data.remove_day_ticket(conn, did, ticket_id, now)
   147	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   148	
   149	
   150	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept")
   151	async def plan_accept(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   152	                      clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   153	    reject_agents(ctx)
   154	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   155	    now = clk.now_unix()
   156	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   157	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   158	    if isinstance(node, int):
   159	        _require_child(tree, node)
   160	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_node(tree, node)
   161	    with txn(conn):
   162	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   163	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   164	
   165	
   166	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept-all")
   167	async def plan_accept_all(date: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   168	    reject_agents(ctx)
   169	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   170	    now = clk.now_unix()
   171	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   172	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_all(tree)
   173	    with txn(conn):
   174	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   175	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   176	
   177	
   178	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/invalidate")
   179	async def plan_invalidate(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   180	                          clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   181	    reject_agents(ctx)
   182	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   183	    now = clk.now_unix()
   184	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   185	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   186	    if isinstance(node, int):
   187	        _require_child(tree, node)
   188	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_child(tree, node)
   189	    else:
   190	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_root(tree)
   191	    with txn(conn):
   192	        replans = days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   193	    for request in replans:  # post-commit enqueue: the R5 consumer sees committed state only
   194	        submit_replan(did, request)
   195	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   196	
   197	
   198	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/reject-all")
   199	async def plan_reject_all(date: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   200	    reject_agents(ctx)
   201	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   202	    now = clk.now_unix()
   203	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   204	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.reject_all(tree)
   205	    with txn(conn):
   206	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   207	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

     1	"""The boundary deterministic pass (§6.2) as pure functions over plain projected
     2	row data. Produce the ``BoundaryInputs`` digest lists: carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals — plus the yesterday done/not-done split for ``day_closed``. Zero DB
     4	access; the boundary layer does the SQL and hands rows in. Stdlib + planner
     5	contract modules only."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from planner.sprints.contracts import ItemStatus
    12	from planner.tickets.contracts import GATING_FIELD, TicketState
    13	
    14	# §6.2.2 — carryover excludes tickets that are already terminal.
    15	_CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE = {TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped}
    16	# Overdue excludes items that are done or already deferred to next sprint.
    17	_ITEM_CLOSED = {ItemStatus.done, ItemStatus.deferred_next_sprint}
    18	
    19	
    20	def carryover_candidates(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    21	    """§6.2 step 2: yesterday's day-tickets whose ticket state is NOT done/dropped.
    22	
    23	    Input rows carry {id, title, state, priority} (ticket joined to its day-ticket,
    24	    in day-ticket position order). Output digest keeps the same four keys. StrEnum
    25	    members compare equal to their string values, so plain state strings work.
    26	    """
    27	    return [
    28	        {
    29	            "id": row["id"],
    30	            "title": row["title"],
    31	            "state": row["state"],
    32	            "priority": row["priority"],
    33	        }
    34	        for row in yesterday_tickets
    35	        if row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE
    36	    ]
    37	
    38	
    39	def day_ticket_counts(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    40	    """§6.2 step 4: (done_count, not_done_count) over yesterday's day-tickets.
    41	
    42	    done_count = rows with state == done; not_done_count = the rest (dropped and
    43	    every unfinished state). The two sum to len(rows).
    44	    """
    45	    done_count = sum(1 for row in yesterday_tickets if row["state"] == TicketState.done)
    46	    return done_count, len(yesterday_tickets) - done_count
    47	
    48	
    49	def overdue_list(
    50	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]], today_iso: str
    51	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    52	    """§6.2 step 3 (overdue). A ticket is overdue iff deadline is not None and
    53	    deadline < today_iso (strict; due-today is NOT overdue) and state not in
    54	    {done, dropped}. An item is overdue iff deadline < today_iso and status not
    55	    in {done, deferred_next_sprint}. Uniform digest {id, title, state, priority}
    56	    (for items, state = the item status). ISO date strings compare lexically.
    57	    Tickets first, then items."""
    58	    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59	    for row in tickets:
    60	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    61	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE:
    62	            result.append(
    63	                {
    64	                    "id": row["id"],
    65	                    "title": row["title"],
    66	                    "state": row["state"],
    67	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    68	                }
    69	            )
    70	    for row in items:
    71	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    72	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["status"] not in _ITEM_CLOSED:
    73	            result.append(
    74	                {
    75	                    "id": row["id"],
    76	                    "title": row["title"],
    77	                    "state": row["status"],
    78	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    79	                }
    80	            )
    81	    return result
    82	
    83	
    84	def approvals_digest(
    85	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]]
    86	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    87	    """§6.2 step 3 / §4.5 approval-queue digest. Three sources, oldest-pending first:
    88	
    89	    * a ticket with a non-null proposal on its current gating field
    90	      (GATING_FIELD[state]); kind = the gating field name (e.g. "plan"),
    91	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"];
    92	    * a ticket in ``needs_review`` (§4.5); kind = "review", waiting_since =
    93	      the ticket's updated_at (DB-internal proxy for review entry time);
    94	    * a sprint item with a non-null status_proposal; kind = "status",
    95	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"].
    96	
    97	    Digest per base.py: {entity_id, kind, waiting_since}. Ticket rows carry
    98	    {id, state, fields(dict), updated_at}; item rows carry {id, status_proposal}.
    99	    """
   100	    digest: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   101	    for row in tickets:
   102	        state = row["state"]
   103	        if state == TicketState.needs_review:
   104	            digest.append(
   105	                {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "review", "waiting_since": row["updated_at"]}
   106	            )
   107	            continue
   108	        gating = GATING_FIELD.get(TicketState(state))
   109	        if gating is None:
   110	            continue
   111	        slot = row["fields"].get(gating.value)
   112	        if slot is None:
   113	            continue
   114	        proposal = slot.get("proposal")
   115	        if proposal is None:
   116	            continue
   117	        digest.append(
   118	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": gating.value, "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   119	        )
   120	    for row in items:
   121	        proposal = row["status_proposal"]
   122	        if proposal is None:
   123	            continue
   124	        digest.append(
   125	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "status", "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   126	        )
   127	    digest.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["waiting_since"])
   128	    return digest

     1	"""§7.6 request context and write validation. This is the server-side dependency
     2	that T10/T13 route bodies consume: it reads the X-Plan-* headers, classifies each
     3	request as a claimed agent, a plain agent, or the human, and validates a presented
     4	claim against a ticket's active lease. Lease liveness is decided only by
     5	dispatch.logic.claims — never re-derived here.
     6	
     7	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py
     8	family), not a logic or data module."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import sqlite3
    13	from dataclasses import dataclass
    14	from typing import Final, NoReturn
    15	
    16	from fastapi import Request
    17	
    18	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    19	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    20	from planner.dispatch.logic.claims import has_active_claim, is_expired
    21	
    22	X_PLAN_RUN_ID: Final = "X-Plan-Run-Id"
    23	X_PLAN_CLAIM: Final = "X-Plan-Claim"
    24	X_PLAN_ACTOR: Final = "X-Plan-Actor"
    25	_DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR: Final = "agent"    # §7.6: PLAN_ACTOR default "agent"
    26	_HUMAN_ACTOR: Final = "human"
    27	
    28	
    29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30	class RequestContext:
    31	    actor: str
    32	    run_id: str | None
    33	    claim: str | None
    34	    is_claimed_agent: bool
    35	    is_human: bool
    36	
    37	
    38	def _normalize(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
    39	    """strip(); an empty or whitespace-only header is an absent header."""
    40	    if raw is None:
    41	        return None
    42	    stripped = raw.strip()
    43	    return stripped or None
    44	
    45	
    46	def _classify(run_id: str | None, claim: str | None, actor: str | None) -> RequestContext:
    47	    """Pure classification over the §1.1 truth table; inputs are pre-normalized."""
    48	    if run_id is not None or claim is not None:
    49	        # Any claim header present (whole or half) is a claimed-agent attempt.
    50	        return RequestContext(
    51	            actor=actor if actor is not None else _DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR,
    52	            run_id=run_id,
    53	            claim=claim,
    54	            is_claimed_agent=True,
    55	            is_human=False,
    56	        )
    57	    if actor is not None:
    58	        # Non-dispatched agent context (planner-main chat agent): proposer from PLAN_ACTOR.
    59	        return RequestContext(
    60	            actor=actor,
    61	            run_id=None,
    62	            claim=None,
    63	            is_claimed_agent=False,
    64	            is_human=False,
    65	        )
    66	    # The human: UI requests carry no agent headers.
    67	    return RequestContext(
    68	        actor=_HUMAN_ACTOR,
    69	        run_id=None,
    70	        claim=None,
    71	        is_claimed_agent=False,
    72	        is_human=True,
    73	    )
    74	
    75	
    76	def request_context(request: Request) -> RequestContext:
    77	    """FastAPI dependency: reads the three headers, normalizes, classifies. No DB, no clock."""
    78	    headers = request.headers
    79	    return _classify(
    80	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_RUN_ID)),
    81	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_CLAIM)),
    82	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_ACTOR)),
    83	    )
    84	
    85	
    86	def _reject_stale(
    87	    reason: str,
    88	    message: str,
    89	    ctx: RequestContext,
    90	    ticket_id: str,
    91	    active_claim_present: bool,
    92	    stored_lock: str | None,
    93	    extra: JsonDict | None = None,
    94	) -> NoReturn:
    95	    # Presented values are echoed back — except a presented token equal to the stored
    96	    # claim_lock (the expired/run_mismatch/missing-half paths reach here with a correct
    97	    # token): verbatim it would put the live stored token into a detail that may be
    98	    # logged or displayed, so it is redacted.
    99	    presented_claim = ctx.claim
   100	    if presented_claim is not None and presented_claim == stored_lock:
   101	        presented_claim = "(redacted)"
   102	    detail: JsonDict = {
   103	        "reason": reason,
   104	        "ticket_id": ticket_id,
   105	        "presented_run_id": ctx.run_id,
   106	        "presented_claim": presented_claim,
   107	        "active_claim_present": active_claim_present,
   108	    }
   109	    if extra is not None:
   110	        detail.update(extra)
   111	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.stale_claim, message, detail)
   112	
   113	
   114	def require_claim(
   115	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ctx: RequestContext, ticket_id: str, now: int
   116	) -> None:
   117	    """§7.6 write validation. Checks run in the fixed order (ticket exists → active
   118	    claim → unexpired → token → run id); the first failure raises stale_claim naming
   119	    which check failed. The stored token is never echoed. now is unix seconds."""
   120	    row = conn.execute(
   121	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   122	    ).fetchone()
   123	    if row is None:
   124	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
   125	    claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   126	    claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   127	    active = has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now)
   128	
   129	    if ctx.run_id is None or ctx.claim is None:
   130	        missing = [
   131	            name
   132	            for name, value in ((X_PLAN_RUN_ID, ctx.run_id), (X_PLAN_CLAIM, ctx.claim))
   133	            if value is None
   134	        ]
   135	        _reject_stale(
   136	            "missing_header",
   137	            "claim headers incomplete",
   138	            ctx,
   139	            ticket_id,
   140	            active,
   141	            claim_lock,
   142	            {"missing": missing},
   143	        )
   144	    if claim_lock is None:
   145	        _reject_stale(
   146	            "none_active", "ticket has no active claim", ctx, ticket_id, active, claim_lock
   147	        )
   148	    if is_expired(claim_expires, now):
   149	        _reject_stale(
   150	            "expired",
   151	            "claim expired",
   152	            ctx,
   153	            ticket_id,
   154	            active,
   155	            claim_lock,
   156	            {"claim_expires": claim_expires},
   157	        )
   158	    if ctx.claim != claim_lock:
   159	        _reject_stale(
   160	            "foreign",
   161	            "claim token does not match the ticket's active claim",
   162	            ctx,
   163	            ticket_id,
   164	            active,
   165	            claim_lock,
   166	        )
   167	    run_row = conn.execute(
   168	        "SELECT id FROM runs WHERE ticket_id=? AND status='running' "
   169	        "ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT 1",
   170	        (ticket_id,),
   171	    ).fetchone()
   172	    if run_row is None or str(run_row["id"]) != ctx.run_id:
   173	        _reject_stale(
   174	            "run_mismatch",
   175	            "run id does not match the ticket's running run",
   176	            ctx,
   177	            ticket_id,
   178	            active,
   179	            claim_lock,
   180	        )
   181	    return None
   182	
   183	
   184	def reject_agents(ctx: RequestContext) -> None:
   185	    """(H) routes: any agent-classified request (claimed or plain) is agent_forbidden."""
   186	    if ctx.is_human:
   187	        return None
   188	    raise PlannerError(
   189	        ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
   190	        "human-only action",
   191	        {"actor": ctx.actor, "is_claimed_agent": ctx.is_claimed_agent},
   192	    )

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/tickets/logic/machine.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2

     1	"""State-machine math for §4.2/§4.3/§4.4: linear order indexing, gating and
     2	advance tables, the auto-accept condition, ceiling comparison, and onward-grant
     3	resolution. Pure: contracts/errors + fields_codec only."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
     8	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
     9	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    10	    GATING_FIELD,
    11	    NO_FURTHER,
    12	    STATE_ORDER,
    13	    AtCap,
    14	    FieldName,
    15	    GrantPair,
    16	    NextCeiling,
    17	    TicketFields,
    18	    TicketState,
    19	)
    20	from planner.tickets.logic import fields_codec
    21	
    22	
    23	def state_index(state: TicketState) -> int:
    24	    try:
    25	        return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
    26	    except ValueError as exc:
    27	        raise PlannerError(
    28	            ErrorCode.validation, "state outside the linear order", {"state": state.value}
    29	        ) from exc
    30	
    31	
    32	def is_terminal(state: TicketState) -> bool:
    33	    return state in (TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped)
    34	
    35	
    36	def gating_field(state: TicketState) -> FieldName | None:
    37	    return GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    38	
    39	
    40	def advance_target(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> TicketState:
    41	    if state is TicketState.in_progress and ceiling is TicketState.done:
    42	        return TicketState.done
    43	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET.get(state)
    44	    if target is None:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.validation, "state has no advance target", {"state": state.value}
    47	        )
    48	    return target
    49	
    50	
    51	def auto_accept_target(
    52	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, field: FieldName
    53	) -> TicketState | None:
    54	    if is_terminal(state):
    55	        return None
    56	    if field is not gating_field(state):
    57	        return None
    58	    target = advance_target(state, ceiling)
    59	    if state_index(target) > state_index(ceiling):
    60	        return None
    61	    return target
    62	
    63	
    64	def at_or_beyond_ceiling(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> bool:
    65	    return state_index(state) >= state_index(ceiling)
    66	
    67	
    68	def validate_ceiling(ceiling: TicketState) -> None:
    69	    if ceiling not in STATE_ORDER:
    70	        raise PlannerError(
    71	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "ceiling must be a linear state", {"ceiling": ceiling.value}
    72	        )
    73	
    74	
    75	def resolve_grant(
    76	    new_state: TicketState, next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None, at_cap: AtCap | None
    77	) -> GrantPair:
    78	    if next_ceiling is None or at_cap is None:
    79	        missing: list[str] = []
    80	        if next_ceiling is None:
    81	            missing.append("next_ceiling")
    82	        if at_cap is None:
    83	            missing.append("at_cap")
    84	        raise PlannerError(
    85	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "accept requires the onward grant pair", {"missing": missing}
    86	        )
    87	    if next_ceiling == NO_FURTHER:
    88	        # the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state
    89	        return GrantPair(next_ceiling=new_state, at_cap=at_cap)
    90	    if not isinstance(next_ceiling, TicketState):
    91	        raise PlannerError(
    92	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": str(next_ceiling)}
    93	        )
    94	    if next_ceiling not in STATE_ORDER or state_index(next_ceiling) < state_index(new_state):
    95	        raise PlannerError(
    96	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid,
    97	            "next_ceiling must be at or beyond the new state",
    98	            {"next_ceiling": next_ceiling.value, "new_state": new_state.value},
    99	        )
   100	    return GrantPair(next_ceiling=next_ceiling, at_cap=at_cap)
   101	
   102	
   103	def has_pending_gating_proposal(state: TicketState, fields: TicketFields) -> bool:
   104	    field = gating_field(state)
   105	    if field is None:
   106	        return False
   107	    return fields_codec.get_slot(fields, field).proposal is not None

     1	"""Ticket domain shapes: the state machine order, the four fields, the grant pair,
     2	and the ticket row. Stdlib only; Priority/Project are imported from core (shared
     3	vocabulary), never redeclared."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8	from enum import StrEnum
     9	from typing import Final, Literal
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import Priority, Project
    12	
    13	
    14	class TicketState(StrEnum):        # §4.1, exact order
    15	    needs_success = "needs_success"
    16	    needs_approach = "needs_approach"
    17	    needs_plan = "needs_plan"
    18	    in_progress = "in_progress"
    19	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    20	    done = "done"
    21	    dropped = "dropped"            # terminal, human-only, outside the linear order
    22	
    23	
    24	# Linear pipeline order (dropped excluded). Index comparison implements "<= ceiling".
    25	# ceiling and next_ceiling values are restricted to members of this tuple (never dropped).
    26	STATE_ORDER: Final[tuple[TicketState, ...]] = (
    27	    TicketState.needs_success, TicketState.needs_approach, TicketState.needs_plan,
    28	    TicketState.in_progress, TicketState.needs_review, TicketState.done,
    29	)
    30	
    31	
    32	class FieldName(StrEnum):          # §4.2 — the exactly-four field keys
    33	    success = "success"
    34	    approach = "approach"
    35	    plan = "plan"
    36	    result = "result"
    37	
    38	
    39	class AtCap(StrEnum):              # §4.3
    40	    stop = "stop"
    41	    propose = "propose"
    42	
    43	
    44	# §4.2 table — gating field per pre-terminal state. needs_review has none (human approve).
    45	GATING_FIELD: Final[dict[TicketState, FieldName]] = {
    46	    TicketState.needs_success: FieldName.success,
    47	    TicketState.needs_approach: FieldName.approach,
    48	    TicketState.needs_plan: FieldName.plan,
    49	    TicketState.in_progress: FieldName.result,
    50	}
    51	
    52	# §4.2 table — accepted proposal advances to. in_progress -> needs_review is the
    53	# default route; the ceiling=done special case (§4.4.5) is resolution-engine logic,
    54	# not a second table entry.
    55	ADVANCE_TARGET: Final[dict[TicketState, TicketState]] = {
    56	    TicketState.needs_success: TicketState.needs_approach,
    57	    TicketState.needs_approach: TicketState.needs_plan,
    58	    TicketState.needs_plan: TicketState.in_progress,
    59	    TicketState.in_progress: TicketState.needs_review,
    60	}
    61	
    62	
    63	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    64	class Proposal:                    # §4.2 proposal slot
    65	    body: str

    67	    created_at: int
    68	
    69	
    70	@dataclass
    71	class FieldSlot:                   # §4.2 — one of the four field objects
    72	    value: str | None = None       # canonical; resolution engine is the only writer
    73	    proposal: Proposal | None = None
    74	    notes: str | None = None       # free guidance; result.notes = review notes (§4.2)

    81	    plan: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    82	    result: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    83	
    84	
    85	# --- the grant pair (§4.4.7) ---
    86	NO_FURTHER: Final = "none"                     # wire sentinel: ceiling = the newly entered state
    87	NextCeiling = TicketState | Literal["none"]    # valid TicketState values are STATE_ORDER members
    88

   114	    claim_lock: str | None         # §7.3 claim token; NULL = unclaimed

   117	    updated_at: int

    65	async def db_conn(request: Request) -> AsyncIterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    66	    conn = cast(Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection], request.app.state.conn_factory)()
    67	    try:
    68	        yield conn
    69	    finally:
    70	        conn.close()
    71	
    72	
    73	DbConn = Annotated[sqlite3.Connection, Depends(db_conn)]
    74	Ctx = Annotated[RequestContext, Depends(request_context)]
    75	Cfg = Annotated[Config, Depends(get_config)]
    76	Clk = Annotated[Clock, Depends(get_clock)]

    78	
    79	@contextmanager
    80	def txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    81	    """Wrap a NON-self-transacting writer (days/dispatch/links) so a mid-sequence
    82	    PlannerError rolls back cleanly. Never wrap a ticket/sprint writer — those open
    83	    their own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and would raise 'transaction within a transaction'."""
    84	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    85	    try:
    86	        yield
    87	    except BaseException:
    88	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    89	        raise
    90	    else:
    91	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    92	
    93	
    94	def parse_enum[E: StrEnum](enum_cls: type[E], raw: str, what: str) -> E:
    95	    try:
    96	        return enum_cls(raw)
    97	    except ValueError:
    98	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {what}", {what: raw}) from None

   149	def _parse_next_ceiling(raw: str | None) -> NextCeiling | None:
   150	    if raw is None:
   151	        return None
   152	    if raw == NO_FURTHER:
   153	        return NO_FURTHER
   154	    try:
   155	        return TicketState(raw)
   156	    except ValueError:
   157	        raise PlannerError(
   158	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": raw}
   159	        ) from None
   160	
   161	
   162	def _parse_grant_at_cap(raw: str | None) -> AtCap | None:
   163	    if raw is None:
   164	        return None
   165	    try:
   166	        return AtCap(raw)
   167	    except ValueError:
   168	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": raw}) from None

   263	@router.patch("/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   264	async def patch_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   265	                       cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   266	    recognized = ("title", "priority", "deadline", "project", "sprint_id")
   267	    for key in body:
   268	        if key not in recognized:
   269	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown ticket field", {"field": key})
   270	    if not body:
   271	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no ticket fields to update", {})
   272	    now = clk.now_unix()
   273	    if "title" in body:
   274	        _set_title(conn, ticket_id, body["title"], title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars, now=now)
   275	    if "priority" in body:
   276	        priority = parse_enum(Priority, body["priority"], "priority")
   277	        tickets_data.set_priority(conn, ticket_id, priority=priority, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   278	    if "deadline" in body:
   279	        tickets_data.set_deadline(conn, ticket_id, deadline=body["deadline"], actor=ctx.actor,
   280	                                  now=now)
   281	    if "project" in body:
   282	        project = parse_enum(Project, body["project"], "project") \
   283	            if body["project"] is not None else None
   284	        _set_project(conn, ticket_id, project, now=now)
   285	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   286	        tickets_data.set_sprint(conn, ticket_id, sprint_id=body["sprint_id"], actor=ctx.actor,
   287	                                now=now)
   288	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id), now)

   372	        ) from None
   373	    try:
   374	        at_cap = AtCap(body.at_cap)
   375	    except ValueError:
   376	        raise PlannerError(
   377	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": body.at_cap}
   378	        ) from None
   379	    ticket = tickets_data.change_grant(
   380	        conn, ticket_id, ceiling=ceiling, at_cap=at_cap, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   381	    )
   382	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

     1	"""§4.4 resolution semantics as pure Decisions. _accept_gating_proposal is the
     2	sole constructor of any Decision that traverses the gating-acceptance edges 1-5
     3	(auto and human alike); _state_change is the sole constructor of state_changed
     4	EventSpecs. Together with data._apply_decision (the sole appender) this is the
     5	"one canonical writer per transition" guarantee (§4.4.6)."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.tickets.contracts import (

    26	CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "auto_accept"
    27	CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "human_accept"
    28	CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE: Final[str] = "review_approve"
    29	CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP: Final[str] = "human_state_jump"
    30	CAUSE_DROP: Final[str] = "drop"
    31	CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT: Final[str] = "onward_grant"
    32	CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT: Final[str] = "human_grant"
    33	RESOLVED_BY_AUTO: Final[str] = "auto"

    36	
    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )

   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

    91	def list_tickets(
    92	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    93	    now: int,
    94	    *,
    95	    state: TicketState | None,
    96	    project: Project | None,
    97	    sprint_id: str | None,
    98	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
    99	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   100	    clauses: list[str] = []
   101	    params: list[str] = []
   102	    if state is not None:
   103	        clauses.append("state = ?")
   104	        params.append(state.value)
   105	    if project is not None:
   106	        clauses.append("project = ?")

   108	    if sprint_id is not None:

   112	        clauses.append("sprint_item_id = ?")
   113	        params.append(sprint_item_id)
   114	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   115	    rows = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT id FROM tickets" + where + " ORDER BY created_at ASC, id", tuple(params)
   117	    ).fetchall()
   118	    return [ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, str(row["id"])), now) for row in rows]

   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)

   131	    ).fetchall()

   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]

   137	    running = conn.execute(

   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(

   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(

   157	            },

   343	            "title": title_by_id.get(c.ticket_id, ""),

   351	
   352	def _overdue(
   353	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, today_iso: str, item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict]
   354	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   355	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   356	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline FROM tickets WHERE deadline IS NOT NULL "
   357	        "ORDER BY deadline ASC, id"
   358	    ).fetchall()
   359	    ticket_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [
   360	        {
   361	            "id": str(r["id"]),
   362	            "title": str(r["title"]),
   363	            "state": str(r["state"]),
   364	            "priority": str(r["priority"]),
   365	            "deadline": str(r["deadline"]) if r["deadline"] is not None else None,
   366	        }
   367	        for r in ticket_rows
   368	    ]
   369	    item_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [dict(r) for r in item_overdue_rows]
   370	    digest = overdue_list(ticket_dicts, item_dicts, today_iso)
   371	    ticket_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in ticket_dicts}
   372	    item_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in item_dicts}
   373	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   374	    for entry in digest:
   375	        eid = str(entry["id"])
   376	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   377	        deadline = ticket_deadline.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_deadline.get(eid)
   378	        result.append(
   379	            {
   380	                "id": eid,
   381	                "entity_type": etype,
   382	                "title": entry["title"],
   383	                "state": entry["state"],
   384	                "priority": entry["priority"],
   385	                "deadline": deadline,
   386	            }
   387	        )
   388	    return result
   389	
   390	
   391	def queues_view(
   392	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   393	    now: int,
   394	    today_iso: str,
   395	    item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict],
   396	    item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict],
   397	) -> JsonDict:
   398	    return {
   399	        "approvals": _approvals(conn, item_approval_rows),
   400	        "pickup": _pickup(conn, now),
   401	        "overdue": _overdue(conn, today_iso, item_overdue_rows),
   402	    }

    80	def _create_idea(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, body: str, project: Project | None,
    81	                 now: int) -> JsonDict:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "idea title is required", {})
    84	    idea_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["idea"])
    85	    with txn(conn):
    86	        conn.execute(
    87	            "INSERT INTO ideas (id, title, body, project, created_at, updated_at) "
    88	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    89	            (idea_id, title, body, project.value if project is not None else None, now, now),
    90	        )
    91	        append_event(conn, idea_id, EventKind.idea_created, {"title": title, "source": "api"}, now)
    92	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM ideas WHERE id = ?", (idea_id,)).fetchone()
    93	    assert row is not None
    94	    return sprints_views.idea_json(row)

    97	def _set_sprint_dates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, date_start: str | None,
    98	                      date_end: str | None, clock: Clock) -> None:
    99	    sprint = sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   100	    new_start = date_start if date_start is not None else sprint.date_start
   101	    new_end = date_end if date_end is not None else sprint.date_end
   102	    for label, value in (("date_start", new_start), ("date_end", new_end)):
   103	        try:
   104	            date.fromisoformat(value)
   105	        except ValueError as exc:
   106	            raise PlannerError(
   107	                ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {label}", {label: value}
   108	            ) from exc
   109	    if new_start > new_end:
   110	        raise PlannerError(
   111	            ErrorCode.validation,
   112	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   113	            {"date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   114	        )
   115	    others = [
   116	        DateRange(id=str(r["id"]), date_start=str(r["date_start"]), date_end=str(r["date_end"]))
   117	        for r in conn.execute(
   118	            "SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints WHERE id != ?", (sprint_id,)
   119	        ).fetchall()
   120	    ]
   121	    conflict = find_overlap(new_start, new_end, others)
   122	    if conflict is not None:
   123	        raise PlannerError(
   124	            ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   125	            "sprint dates overlap",
   126	            {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   127	        )
   128	    now = clock.now_unix()
   129	    with txn(conn):
   130	        conn.execute(
   131	            "UPDATE sprints SET date_start = ?, date_end = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   132	            (new_start, new_end, now, sprint_id),
   133	        )
   134	        if new_start != sprint.date_start:
   135	            append_event(
   136	                conn,
   137	                sprint_id,
   138	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   139	                {"field": "date_start", "from": sprint.date_start, "to": new_start},
   140	                now,
   141	            )
   142	        if new_end != sprint.date_end:
   143	            append_event(
   144	                conn,
   145	                sprint_id,
   146	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   147	                {"field": "date_end", "from": sprint.date_end, "to": new_end},
   148	                now,
   149	            )
   150	
   151	
   152	def _marshal_item_deadline(raw: object) -> None:
   153	    if raw is None:
   154	        return
   155	    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   156	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw})
   157	    try:
   158	        date.fromisoformat(raw)
   159	    except ValueError as exc:
   160	        raise PlannerError(
   161	            ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw}
   162	        ) from exc

   169	async def create_item(body: CreateItemBody, conn: DbConn, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   170	    if body.project is None:
   171	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is required")
   172	    project = parse_enum(Project, body.project, "project")
   173	    priority = parse_enum(Priority, body.priority, "priority") if body.priority is not None \
   174	        else Priority.P3

   177	        conn,
   178	        title=body.title,
   179	        project=project,
   180	        body=body.body,
   181	        priority=priority,
   182	        deadline=body.deadline,
   183	        current_state_note=body.current_state_note,
   184	        sprint_id=body.sprint_id,
   185	        clock=clk,
   186	    )
   187	    return {**sprints_views.item_json(item), "blockers_cleared": False}

   207	@router.patch("/items/{item_id}")
   208	async def patch_item(item_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   209	                     clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   210	    recognized = set(_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS) | {"sprint_id", "status", "blocked_by"}
   211	    for key in body:
   212	        if key not in recognized:
   213	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown item field", {"field": key})
   214	    if not body:
   215	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no item fields to update", {})
   216	    if "blocked_by" in body and "status" not in body:
   217	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "blocked_by requires status", {})
   218	    for field in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   219	        if field in body:
   220	            if field == "deadline":
   221	                _marshal_item_deadline(body["deadline"])
   222	            sprints_data.update_item_field(conn, item_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   223	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   224	        sprints_data.assign_item_sprint(conn, item_id, body["sprint_id"], clock=clk)
   225	    if "status" in body:
   226	        to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body["status"], "status")
   227	        sprints_data.transition_item_status(
   228	            conn, item_id, to_status, clock=clk, by_agent=not ctx.is_human,
   229	            blocked_by=body.get("blocked_by"),
   230	        )
   231	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   232	
   233	
   234	@router.post("/items/{item_id}/propose-status")
   235	async def propose_item_status(item_id: str, body: ProposeStatusBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,

   184	def copy_text(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str:
   185	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   186	    fields = ticket.fields
   187	
   188	    def show(value: str | None) -> str:
   189	        return value if value else "(none)"
   190	
   191	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   192	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   193	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   194	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   195	    ).fetchall()
   196	    if link_rows:
   197	        links_block = "\n".join(
   198	            f"- {str(r['kind'])}: {str(r['from_id'])} -> {str(r['to_id'])}" for r in link_rows
   199	        )
   200	    else:
   201	        links_block = "(none)"
   202	    return (
   203	        f"{ticket.title}\n"
   204	        f"state: {ticket.state.value}\n"
   205	        f"priority: {ticket.priority.value}\n"
   206	        f"\n"
   207	        f"success:\n{show(fields.success.value)}\n"
   208	        f"\n"
   209	        f"approach:\n{show(fields.approach.value)}\n"
   210	        f"\n"
   211	        f"plan:\n{show(fields.plan.value)}\n"
   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )
