   221	# --- board (§10.3) -------------------------------------------------------------
   222	
   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}
   260	
   261	
   262	# --- queues (§4.5) -------------------------------------------------------------
   263	
   264	
   265	def _entity_type(entity_id: str) -> str:
   266	    return "ticket" if entity_id.split("_", 1)[0] == "t" else "item"
   267	
   268	
   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()
   274	    ticket_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   275	    ticket_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   276	    ticket_updated: dict[str, int] = {}
   277	    for r in ticket_rows:
   278	        tid = str(r["id"])
   279	        ticket_digest.append(
   280	            {
   281	                "id": tid,
   282	                "state": str(r["state"]),
   283	                "fields": json.loads(str(r["fields"])),
   284	                "updated_at": int(r["updated_at"]),
   285	            }
   286	        )
   287	        ticket_title[tid] = str(r["title"])
   288	        ticket_updated[tid] = int(r["updated_at"])
   289	    item_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   290	    item_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   291	    for r in item_approval_rows:
   292	        iid = str(r["id"])
   293	        item_digest.append({"id": iid, "status_proposal": r["status_proposal"]})
   294	        item_title[iid] = str(r["title"])
   295	    digest = approvals_digest(ticket_digest, item_digest)
   296	    # A5: review entries use the last state_changed->needs_review event time, not the
   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.
   298	    for entry in digest:
   299	        if entry["kind"] == "review":
   300	            tid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   301	            row = conn.execute(
   302	                "SELECT created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? AND kind = 'state_changed' "
   303	                "AND json_extract(payload, '$.to') = 'needs_review' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],
   321	                "title": title,
   322	                "waiting_since": entry["waiting_since"],
   323	            }
   324	        )
   325	    return result
   326	
   327	
   328	def _pickup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   329	    candidates = dispatch_data.load_candidates(conn, now)
   330	    eligible = [c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)]
   331	    eligible.sort(key=ordering_key)
   332	    if not eligible:
   333	        return []
   334	    ids = [c.ticket_id for c in eligible]
   335	    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)
   336	    rows = conn.execute(
   337	        f"SELECT id, title FROM tickets WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", tuple(ids)
   338	    ).fetchall()
   339	    title_by_id = {str(r["id"]): str(r["title"]) for r in rows}
   340	    return [

     1	"""The chat passthrough (§11). Resolves the chat entity (ticket or day), sends
     2	through the gateway adapter, and — on the first reply only — persists the minted
     3	session key onto the entity and logs one chat_session_created event in a single
     4	transaction. Framework-free: no FastAPI/pydantic (SPEC §14 / server.py docstring).
     5	Times arrive as unix-second ints from the caller's clock."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import logging
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from collections.abc import Iterator
    12	from contextlib import contextmanager
    13	from datetime import date
    14	
    15	from planner.chat.contracts import ChatSendResult, GatewayStatus
    16	from planner.core.adapters.base import GatewayAdapter
    17	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    18	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    19	from planner.core.events import append_event
    20	from planner.days.data import read_day
    21	
    22	_log = logging.getLogger("planner.chat")
    23	
    24	
    25	@contextmanager
    26	def _txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    27	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    28	    try:
    29	        yield
    30	    except BaseException:
    31	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    32	        raise
    33	    else:
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	
    36	
    37	def _resolve(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str, now: int) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    38	    if entity_id.startswith("t_"):
    39	        row = conn.execute(
    40	            "SELECT chat_session_key FROM tickets WHERE id = ?", (entity_id,)
    41	        ).fetchone()
    42	        if row is None:
    43	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"entity_id": entity_id})
    44	        key: str | None = row["chat_session_key"]
    45	        return "ticket", key
    46	    if entity_id.startswith("day_"):
    47	        # A day id is a canonical YYYY-MM-DD; round-trip the suffix because
    48	        # fromisoformat alone accepts compact variants. A non-canonical id names
    49	        # no day, so it is not_found rather than a materialized empty day.
    50	        raw = entity_id[len("day_") :]
    51	        try:
    52	            canonical = date.fromisoformat(raw).isoformat() == raw
    53	        except ValueError:
    54	            canonical = False
    55	        if not canonical:
    56	            raise PlannerError(
    57	                ErrorCode.not_found, "no chattable entity for id", {"entity_id": entity_id}
    58	            )
    59	        day = read_day(conn, entity_id, now)  # §3.4: materializes if absent
    60	        return "day", day.chat_session_key
    61	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "no chattable entity for id", {"entity_id": entity_id})
    62	
    63	
    64	def send(
    65	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    66	    gateway: GatewayAdapter,
    67	    entity_id: str,
    68	    text: str,
    69	    now: int,
    70	) -> ChatSendResult:
    71	    kind, stored_key = _resolve(conn, entity_id, now)
    72	    # The gateway call is IO; it runs outside any transaction.
    73	    try:
    74	        result = gateway.send(stored_key, entity_id, text)
    75	    except PlannerError:
    76	        raise  # already structured (offline adapter raises gateway_offline)
    77	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
    78	        raise PlannerError(
    79	            ErrorCode.gateway_offline, "gateway unavailable", {"cause": str(exc)}
    80	        ) from exc
    81	    if stored_key is None:  # first reply → persist the key and log one event
    82	        table = "tickets" if kind == "ticket" else "days"  # fixed map, never request input
    83	        with _txn(conn):
    84	            cursor = conn.execute(
    85	                f"UPDATE {table} SET chat_session_key = ?, updated_at = ? "
    86	                "WHERE id = ? AND chat_session_key IS NULL",
    87	                (result.session_key, now, entity_id),
    88	            )
    89	            if cursor.rowcount == 1:
    90	                append_event(
    91	                    conn,
    92	                    entity_id,
    93	                    EventKind.chat_session_created,
    94	                    {"session_key": result.session_key},
    95	                    now,
    96	                )
    97	            else:  # a concurrent first send won under the write lock; adopt its key
    98	                row = conn.execute(
    99	                    f"SELECT chat_session_key FROM {table} WHERE id = ?", (entity_id,)
   100	                ).fetchone()
   101	                result = ChatSendResult(
   102	                    reply_text=result.reply_text, session_key=row["chat_session_key"]
   103	                )
   104	    return result
   105	
   106	
   107	def status(gateway: GatewayAdapter) -> GatewayStatus:
   108	    result = gateway.status()
   109	    if result.detail is not None:
   110	        _log.info("gateway status detail: %s", result.detail)  # logged, not shown
   111	    return result

    18	_FIRST_FAKE_PID = 90001
    19	
    20	
    21	@dataclass
    22	class FakeSpawnAdapter:
    23	    calls: list[SpawnRequest] = field(default_factory=list)
    24	    results: list[SpawnResult] = field(default_factory=list)  # scripted, popped FIFO
    25	    next_pid: int = _FIRST_FAKE_PID
    26	
    27	    def spawn(self, request: SpawnRequest) -> SpawnResult:
    28	        self.calls.append(request)
    29	        if self.results:
    30	            return self.results.pop(0)
    31	        pid = self.next_pid
    32	        self.next_pid += 1
    33	        return SpawnResult(ok=True, pid=pid)
    34

    55	                position=index,

    58	        ]

    60	        return BoundaryJudgment(
    61	            brief_markdown=f"# Brief for {inputs.planning_date}", plan_tree=tree
    62	        )
    63

    66	        if self.fail:
    67	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    68	        if self.replan_tree is not None:
    69	            return self.replan_tree
    70	        return PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake replanned focus"), children=[])
    71

     1	"""Adapter registry (§9.4). Selects real vs fake (vs offline gateway) per adapter
     2	from config keys. `auto` resolves to fake under test mode and real otherwise;
     3	explicit values always win. An unknown value fails loudly at startup."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass
     8	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9	
    10	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, GatewayAdapter, SpawnAdapter
    11	from planner.core.adapters.fakes import (
    12	    EchoGatewayAdapter,
    13	    FakeBoundaryAdapter,
    14	    FakeSpawnAdapter,
    15	    OfflineGatewayAdapter,
    16	)
    17	from planner.core.adapters.real import (
    18	    RealBoundaryAdapter,
    19	    RealGatewayAdapter,
    20	    RealSpawnAdapter,
    21	)
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	
    24	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    25	    from planner.core.config import Config
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29	class Adapters:
    30	    spawn: SpawnAdapter
    31	    boundary: BoundaryAdapter
    32	    gateway: GatewayAdapter
    33	
    34	
    35	def _resolve_auto(choice: str, test_mode: bool) -> str:
    36	    if choice == "auto":
    37	        return "fake" if test_mode else "real"
    38	    return choice
    39	
    40	
    41	def _select_spawn(config: Config) -> SpawnAdapter:
    42	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.spawn_adapter, config.test_mode)
    43	    if choice == "fake":
    44	        return FakeSpawnAdapter()
    45	    if choice == "real":
    46	        return RealSpawnAdapter(config)
    47	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown spawn_adapter: {config.spawn_adapter!r}")
    48	
    49	
    50	def _select_boundary(config: Config) -> BoundaryAdapter:
    51	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.boundary_adapter, config.test_mode)
    52	    if choice == "fake":
    53	        return FakeBoundaryAdapter()
    54	    if choice == "real":
    55	        return RealBoundaryAdapter(config)
    56	    raise PlannerError(
    57	        ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown boundary_adapter: {config.boundary_adapter!r}"
    58	    )
    59	
    60	
    61	def _select_gateway(config: Config) -> GatewayAdapter:
    62	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.gateway_adapter, config.test_mode)
    63	    if choice == "fake":
    64	        return EchoGatewayAdapter()
    65	    if choice == "offline":
    66	        return OfflineGatewayAdapter()
    67	    if choice == "real":
    68	        return RealGatewayAdapter(config)
    69	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown gateway_adapter: {config.gateway_adapter!r}")
    70	
    71	
    72	def build_adapters(config: Config) -> Adapters:
    73	    return Adapters(
    74	        spawn=_select_spawn(config),
    75	        boundary=_select_boundary(config),
    76	        gateway=_select_gateway(config),
    77	    )

    12	
    13	
    14	class Clock(Protocol):
    15	    def now(self) -> datetime: ...       # timezone-aware, LOCAL time (§6.1 math is local)
    16	    def now_unix(self) -> int: ...
    17	
    18	
    19	class RealClock:
    20	    def now(self) -> datetime:
    21	        return datetime.now().astimezone()
    22	
    23	    def now_unix(self) -> int:
    24	        return int(self.now().timestamp())
    25	
    26	
    27	class TestClock:
    28	    """Mutable clock for PLAN_FAKE_NOW and POST /api/test/set-now (D5)."""
    29	
    30	    def __init__(self, start: datetime) -> None:
    31	        self._now = start
    32	
    33	    def now(self) -> datetime:
    34	        return self._now
    35	
    36	    def now_unix(self) -> int:
    37	        return int(self._now.timestamp())
    38	
    39	    def set(self, now: datetime) -> None:
    40	        self._now = now
    41	
    42	
    43	def parse_fake_now(raw: str) -> datetime:
    44	    """PLAN_FAKE_NOW is ISO; a naive value is interpreted as local time."""
    45	    parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    46	    if parsed.tzinfo is None:
    47	        parsed = parsed.astimezone()
    48	    return parsed
    49	
    50	
    51	def build_clock(config: Config) -> Clock:
    52	    if config.test_mode and config.fake_now:
    53	        return TestClock(parse_fake_now(config.fake_now))
    54	    return RealClock()

     1	"""§9/§13 test-mode control router. Mounted under /api only when config.test_mode,
     2	so /api/test/* is a plain 404 otherwise (the router is never built). set-now drives
     3	the TestClock; the two tick endpoints call T11's runtime through a lazy import seam
     4	and answer 501 until it lands.
     5	
     6	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py family)."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import importlib
    11	import sqlite3
    12	from collections.abc import Callable
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from fastapi import APIRouter
    16	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    17	
    18	from planner.core.adapters.registry import Adapters
    19	from planner.core.clock import Clock, TestClock, parse_fake_now
    20	from planner.core.config import Config
    21	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date
    24	
    25	
    26	def _not_wired() -> JSONResponse:
    27	    # Returned directly (never raised as a PlannerError, which the handler would map
    28	    # to 400): the seam pins this exact body for T11's tests to compare whole.
    29	    return JSONResponse(
    30	        status_code=501,
    31	        content={"error": {"code": "validation", "message": "runtime not wired yet"}},
    32	    )
    33	
    34	
    35	def _lazy(module_name: str, attr: str) -> Any | None:
    36	    # importlib (not a from-import) keeps the not-yet-existing T11 modules out of the
    37	    # static import graph; ImportError/AttributeError are the seam's named failures.
    38	    try:
    39	        module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    40	        return getattr(module, attr)
    41	    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    42	        return None
    43	
    44	
    45	def build_test_router(
    46	    config: Config,
    47	    clock: Clock,
    48	    adapters: Adapters,
    49	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    50	) -> APIRouter:
    51	    router = APIRouter()
    52	
    53	    @router.post("/test/set-now")
    54	    async def set_now(body: dict[str, Any]) -> JsonDict:
    55	        raw = body.get("now")
    56	        if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    57	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "body must carry an ISO 'now' string")
    58	        try:
    59	            parsed = parse_fake_now(raw)
    60	        except ValueError as exc:
    61	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid ISO datetime: {raw!r}") from exc
    62	        if not isinstance(clock, TestClock):
    63	            raise PlannerError(
    64	                ErrorCode.validation, "clock is not a TestClock; start with PLAN_FAKE_NOW set"
    65	            )
    66	        clock.set(parsed)
    67	        return {
    68	            "now": clock.now().isoformat(),
    69	            "planning_date": planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour).isoformat(),
    70	        }
    71	
    72	    @router.post("/test/tick-boundary")
    73	    async def tick_boundary() -> JSONResponse:
    74	        fn = _lazy("planner.days.scheduler", "run_boundary_tick")
    75	        if fn is None:
    76	            return _not_wired()
    77	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    78	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)
    79	
    80	    @router.post("/test/tick-dispatcher")
    81	    async def tick_dispatcher() -> JSONResponse:
    82	        fn = _lazy("planner.dispatch.runtime", "run_tick")
    83	        if fn is None:
    84	            return _not_wired()
    85	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    86	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)
    87	
    88	    return router

     1	"""Configuration: every tunable lives here, loaded from config.yaml with PLAN_*
     2	environment overrides. Test-mode flags are environment-only and never in the
     3	checked-in yaml. Nothing in the system reads a timing/limit/path inline."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	import os
     8	from collections.abc import Mapping
     9	from dataclasses import dataclass
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	from typing import Final
    12	
    13	import yaml
    14	
    15	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    16	
    17	HOST: Final = "127.0.0.1"                    # §2: the bind is fixed, not tunable
    18	DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH: Final = "config.yaml"
    19	
    20	_TRUE: Final = frozenset({"1", "true", "yes", "on"})
    21	_FALSE: Final = frozenset({"0", "false", "no", "off"})
    22	
    23	
    24	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25	class Config:
    26	    # §13 named keys
    27	    db_path: str
    28	    port: int
    29	    boundary_hour: int
    30	    tick_seconds: int
    31	    claim_ttl_seconds: int
    32	    max_runs: int
    33	    failure_limit: int
    34	    dispatch_enabled: bool
    35	    hermes_bin: str
    36	    hermes_profile: str
    37	    worker_skill: str
    38	    # other tunables named across SPEC
    39	    ws_poll_ms: int
    40	    ui_debounce_ms: int
    41	    run_max_seconds: int
    42	    boundary_timeout_seconds: int
    43	    title_max_chars: int
    44	    dispatcher_lock_path: str
    45	    logs_dir: str
    46	    events_read_limit: int
    47	    db_busy_timeout_ms: int
    48	    # adapter selection (registry §9.4)
    49	    spawn_adapter: str
    50	    boundary_adapter: str
    51	    gateway_adapter: str
    52	    # test mode — ENV ONLY, never in config.yaml
    53	    test_mode: bool
    54	    fake_now: str | None
    55	
    56	
    57	def _parse_bool(raw: object, key: str) -> bool:
    58	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    59	        return raw
    60	    text = str(raw).strip().lower()
    61	    if text in _TRUE:
    62	        return True
    63	    if text in _FALSE:
    64	        return False
    65	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid boolean for {key}: {raw!r}")
    66	
    67	
    68	def _parse_int(raw: object, key: str) -> int:
    69	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    70	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    71	    if isinstance(raw, int):
    72	        return raw
    73	    if isinstance(raw, str):
    74	        try:
    75	            return int(raw.strip())
    76	        except ValueError as exc:
    77	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}") from exc
    78	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    79	
    80	
    81	def _read_yaml(path: str | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    82	    resolved = Path(path) if path is not None else Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH)
    83	    if not resolved.exists():
    84	        return {}
    85	    loaded = yaml.safe_load(resolved.read_text())
    86	    if loaded is None:
    87	        return {}
    88	    if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
    89	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"config root must be a mapping: {resolved}")
    90	    return loaded
    91	
    92	
    93	def _str_value(
    94	    file_cfg: Mapping[str, object], env: Mapping[str, str], key: str, env_var: str, default: str
    95	) -> str:
    96	    value: object = default
    97	    if key in file_cfg:
    98	        value = file_cfg[key]
    99	    if env_var in env:
   100	        value = env[env_var]
   101	    return str(value)
   102	
   103	
   104	def _int_value(
   105	    file_cfg: Mapping[str, object], env: Mapping[str, str], key: str, env_var: str, default: int
   106	) -> int:
   107	    value: object = default
   108	    if key in file_cfg:
   109	        value = file_cfg[key]
   110	    if env_var in env:
   111	        value = env[env_var]
   112	    return _parse_int(value, key)
   113	
   114	
   115	def _bool_value(
   116	    file_cfg: Mapping[str, object], env: Mapping[str, str], key: str, env_var: str, default: bool
   117	) -> bool:
   118	    value: object = default
   119	    if key in file_cfg:
   120	        value = file_cfg[key]
   121	    if env_var in env:
   122	        value = env[env_var]
   123	    return _parse_bool(value, key)
   124

   170	def read_dispatch_enabled(path: str | None = None, env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> bool:
   171	    """§7.1 fail-safe: the dispatcher re-reads this every tick. Any read/parse
   172	    failure returns False (a missing config file is not a failure — defaults apply)."""
   173	    try:

    23	def seed_demo(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    24	    for table in _TABLES:
    25	        if conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}").fetchone()[0] > 0:
    26	            raise PlannerError(
    27	                ErrorCode.db_not_empty, "seed --demo requires an empty database", {"table": table}
    28	            )
    29	    now = int(time.time())
    30	    today = datetime.now().astimezone().date()
    31	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    32	    try:
    33	        _build_demo(conn, now, today)
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	    except Exception:
    36	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    37	        raise

   116	def _insert_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date) -> str:
   117	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])

   126	            "Demo: one clear slice must ship.",
   127	            "Demo: shipping the feature slice unblocks the review queue.",
   128	            "- Demo support line.", "- Demo premortem line.", now, now,
   129	        ),
   130	    )
   131	    append_event(
   132	        conn, sprint_id, EventKind.sprint_created,
   133	        {"name": "Demo Sprint", "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end, "source": "demo"},
   134	        now,
   135	    )
   136	    return sprint_id

   164	def _fields(title: str, keys: tuple[str, ...], result_notes: str | None = None) -> str:
   165	    slots: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {

   187	    sprint_id: str | None,
   188	    project: str | None,

    26	def materialize_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
    27	    """§3.4: create the day row if absent (brief='', notes='', plan=NULL,
    28	    chat_session_key=NULL, created_at=updated_at=now_unix) and append a
    29	    day_created event. Idempotent: a present day → no write, no event."""
    30	    if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone() is not None:
    31	        return
    32	    conn.execute(
    33	        "INSERT INTO days (id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at) "
    34	        "VALUES (?, '', '', NULL, NULL, ?, ?)",
    35	        (day_id, now_unix, now_unix),
    36	    )
    37	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_created, {}, now_unix)
    38	
    39	
    40	def read_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> Day:
    41	    """§3.4 'reading a nonexistent day materializes it empty'. Materializes, then
    42	    SELECTs the row and builds a Day (plan parsed via tree_from_dict when set)."""
    43	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    44	    row = conn.execute(
    45	        "SELECT id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at "
    46	        "FROM days WHERE id = ?",
    47	        (day_id,),
    48	    ).fetchone()
    49	    assert row is not None
    50	    plan_json = row["plan"]
    51	    return Day(
    52	        id=row["id"],
    53	        brief=row["brief"],
    54	        notes=row["notes"],
    55	        plan=tree_from_dict(json.loads(plan_json)) if plan_json is not None else None,
    56	        chat_session_key=row["chat_session_key"],
    57	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    58	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    59	    )

    62	def list_day_tickets(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> list[DayTicket]:
    63	    """day_tickets for the day, ordered by position (contiguous from 0)."""
    64	    rows = conn.execute(
    65	        "SELECT day_id, ticket_id, position FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
    66	        (day_id,),
    67	    ).fetchall()
    68	    return [
    69	        DayTicket(day_id=row["day_id"], ticket_id=row["ticket_id"], position=row["position"])
    70	        for row in rows
    71	    ]

    74	def load_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> PlanTree | None:
    75	    """Parse days.plan JSON → PlanTree, or None (absent day or NULL plan)."""
    76	    row = conn.execute("SELECT plan FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone()
    77	    if row is None or row["plan"] is None:
    78	        return None
    79	    return tree_from_dict(json.loads(row["plan"]))

    82	def store_plan(
    83	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, tree: PlanTree | None, now_unix: int
    84	) -> None:
    85	    """Materialize, then write days.plan = tree_to_dict(tree) JSON (or NULL) and
    86	    bump updated_at. No event here (callers emit the specific plan_* event)."""
    87	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    88	    plan_json = json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)) if tree is not None else None
    89	    conn.execute(
    90	        "UPDATE days SET plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
    91	        (plan_json, now_unix, day_id),
    92	    )

    97	) -> None:
    98	    """Boundary success path: write days.brief + days.plan (serialized) +
    99	    updated_at in one UPDATE. No day_updated event (that kind is reserved for
   100	    manual edits); the boundary emits plan_proposed separately."""
   101	    conn.execute(
   102	        "UPDATE days SET brief = ?, plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   103	        (brief, json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)), now_unix, day_id),

   107	def add_day_ticket(
   108	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int, cause: str = "manual"
   109	) -> bool:
   110	    """§3.4: append at end with the next contiguous position (= current count).
   111	    Idempotent: if (day_id, ticket_id) already present → return False, no event.
   112	    On add → INSERT, append day_ticket_added {ticket_id, position, cause}, bump
   113	    updated_at, return True. Materializes the day first (write path)."""
   114	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   115	    present = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT 1 FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   117	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   118	    ).fetchone()
   119	    if present is not None:
   120	        return False
   121	    position = conn.execute(
   122	        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ?", (day_id,)
   123	    ).fetchone()["n"]
   124	    conn.execute(
   125	        "INSERT INTO day_tickets (day_id, ticket_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   126	        (day_id, ticket_id, position),
   127	    )
   128	    append_event(
   129	        conn,
   130	        day_id,
   131	        EventKind.day_ticket_added,
   132	        {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "position": position, "cause": cause},
   133	        now_unix,
   134	    )
   135	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   136	    return True
   137	
   138	
   139	def remove_day_ticket(
   140	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int
   141	) -> None:
   142	    """§3.4: delete the association only (ticket state untouched). If nothing was
   143	    deleted (rowcount 0) → no-op, no event, no re-pack. Else re-pack remaining
   144	    positions to 0..n-1 in existing position order, append day_ticket_removed
   145	    {ticket_id}, bump updated_at."""
   146	    cursor = conn.execute(
   147	        "DELETE FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   148	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   149	    )
   150	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   151	        return
   152	    survivors = conn.execute(
   153	        "SELECT ticket_id FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
   154	        (day_id,),
   155	    ).fetchall()
   156	    for new_position, row in enumerate(survivors):
   157	        conn.execute(
   158	            "UPDATE day_tickets SET position = ? WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   159	            (new_position, day_id, row["ticket_id"]),
   160	        )
   161	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_ticket_removed, {"ticket_id": ticket_id}, now_unix)
   162	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   163

    61	def free_or(preferred: int) -> int:
    62	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as probe:
    63	        try:
    64	            probe.bind(("127.0.0.1", preferred))
    65	            return preferred
    66	        except OSError:
    67	            pass
    68	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as fallback:
    69	        fallback.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    70	        return int(fallback.getsockname()[1])
    71

    73	def wait_ready(base: str, budget: float = 15.0, interval: float = 0.25) -> None:
    74	    deadline = time.time() + budget
    75	    while time.time() < deadline:
    76	        try:
    77	            if httpx.get(f"{base}/api/meta", timeout=1.0).status_code == 200:
    78	                return
    79	        except httpx.HTTPError:
    80	            pass
    81	        time.sleep(interval)
    82	    raise AssertionError("server did not become ready within budget")

    71	        limiting_factor=row["limiting_factor"],
    72	        primary_bet=row["primary_bet"],
    73	        supports=row["supports"],
    74	        premortem=row["premortem"],
    75	        weekly_addenda=[Addendum(**a) for a in json.loads(row["weekly_addenda"])],
    76	        kickoff_frozen_at=row["kickoff_frozen_at"],
    77	        outcomes=row["outcomes"],
    78	        solo_reflection=row["solo_reflection"],
    79	        joint_discussion=row["joint_discussion"],
    80	        updates_to_thinking=row["updates_to_thinking"],
    81	        carry_forward=row["carry_forward"],
    82	        review_frozen_at=row["review_frozen_at"],
    83	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    84	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    85	    )

    87	
    88	def _proposal_to_json(p: ItemStatusProposal) -> str:
    89	    return json.dumps(
    90	        {
    91	            "to_status": p.to_status.value,
    92	            "note": p.note,
    93	            "proposed_by": p.proposed_by,
    94	            "created_at": p.created_at,
    95	        }
    96	    )
    97	
    98	
    99	def _proposal_from_json(raw: str | None) -> ItemStatusProposal | None:
   100	    if raw is None:
   101	        return None
   102	    data = json.loads(raw)
   103	    return ItemStatusProposal(
   104	        to_status=ItemStatus(data["to_status"]),
   105	        note=data["note"],
   106	        proposed_by=data["proposed_by"],
   107	        created_at=data["created_at"],
   108	    )
   109	
   110	
   111	def _row_to_item(row: sqlite3.Row) -> SprintItem:
   112	    return SprintItem(
   113	        id=row["id"],
   114	        title=row["title"],
   115	        body=row["body"],
   116	        status=ItemStatus(row["status"]),
   117	        priority=Priority(row["priority"]),
   118	        deadline=row["deadline"],
   119	        project=Project(row["project"]),
   120	        current_state_note=row["current_state_note"],
   121	        sprint_id=row["sprint_id"],
   122	        blocked_by=json.loads(row["blocked_by"]),
   123	        status_proposal=_proposal_from_json(row["status_proposal"]),
   124	        created_at=row["created_at"],
   125	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
   126	    )
   127	
   128	
   129	def _load_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str) -> Sprint:
   130	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM sprints WHERE id = ?", (sprint_id,)).fetchone()
   131	    if row is None:
   132	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint not found", {"id": sprint_id})
   133	    return _row_to_sprint(row)
   134	
   135	
   136	def _load_item(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> SprintItem:
   137	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (item_id,)).fetchone()
   138	    if row is None:
   139	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint item not found", {"id": item_id})
   140	    return _row_to_item(row)
   141	
   142	
   143	# --- sprint writers -------------------------------------------------------------
   144	
   145	
   146	def create_sprint(
   147	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   148	    *,
   149	    name: str,
   150	    date_start: str,
   151	    date_end: str,
   152	    limiting_factor: str = "",
   153	    primary_bet: str = "",
   154	    supports: str = "",
   155	    premortem: str = "",
   156	    clock: Clock,
   157	) -> Sprint:
   158	    if not name:
   159	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "sprint name is required", {})
   160	    if date_start > date_end:
   161	        raise PlannerError(
   162	            ErrorCode.validation,
   163	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   164	            {"date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   165	        )
   166	    now = clock.now_unix()
   167	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])
   168	    with _tx(conn):
   169	        existing = [
   170	            DateRange(id=r["id"], date_start=r["date_start"], date_end=r["date_end"])
   171	            for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints")
   172	        ]
   173	        conflict = find_overlap(date_start, date_end, existing)
   174	        if conflict is not None:
   175	            raise PlannerError(
   176	                ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   177	                "sprint dates overlap",
   178	                {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   179	            )
   180	        conn.execute(
   181	            "INSERT INTO sprints ("
   182	            "id, name, date_start, date_end, limiting_factor, primary_bet, supports, "
   183	            "premortem, weekly_addenda, kickoff_frozen_at, outcomes, solo_reflection, "
   184	            "joint_discussion, updates_to_thinking, carry_forward, review_frozen_at, "
   185	            "created_at, updated_at) "
   186	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '[]', NULL, '', '', '', '', '', NULL, ?, ?)",
   187	            (
   188	                sprint_id,
   189	                name,
   190	                date_start,
   191	                date_end,
   192	                limiting_factor,
   193	                primary_bet,
   194	                supports,
   195	                premortem,
   196	                now,
   197	                now,
   198	            ),
   199	        )
   200	        append_event(
   201	            conn,
   202	            sprint_id,
   203	            EventKind.sprint_created,
   204	            {"name": name, "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   205	            now,
   206	        )
   207	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   208	
   209	
   210	def update_sprint_field(
   211	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, field: str, value: str, *, clock: Clock
   212	) -> Sprint:
   213	    if field not in _SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS:
   214	        raise PlannerError(
   215	            ErrorCode.validation,
   216	            "field is not an editable sprint text field",
   217	            {"field": field},
   218	        )
   219	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   220	    if not field_write_admissible(field, sprint.kickoff_frozen_at, sprint.review_frozen_at):
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.frozen_write,
   223	            "field is frozen",
   224	            {"field": field, "group": frozen_group(field)},
   225	        )
   226	    now = clock.now_unix()
   227	    prev = getattr(sprint, field)
   228	    with _tx(conn):
   229	        conn.execute(
   230	            f"UPDATE sprints SET {field} = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   231	            (value, now, sprint_id),
   232	        )
   233	        append_event(
   234	            conn,
   235	            sprint_id,
   236	            EventKind.sprint_updated,
   237	            {"field": field, "from": prev, "to": value},
   238	            now,
   239	        )
   240	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   241	
   242	
   243	def freeze_kickoff(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, clock: Clock) -> Sprint:
   244	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   245	    if sprint.kickoff_frozen_at is not None:
   246	        return sprint
   247	    now = clock.now_unix()
   248	    with _tx(conn):
   249	        # Latch inside the transaction: only the write that flips NULL emits the event.
   250	        cursor = conn.execute(
   251	            "UPDATE sprints SET kickoff_frozen_at = ?, updated_at = ? "
   252	            "WHERE id = ? AND kickoff_frozen_at IS NULL",
   253	            (now, now, sprint_id),
   254	        )
   255	        if cursor.rowcount == 1:
   256	            append_event(conn, sprint_id, EventKind.kickoff_frozen, {"frozen_at": now}, now)
   257	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   258	
   259

   350	def update_item_field(
   351	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str, field: str, value: str | None, *, clock: Clock
   352	) -> SprintItem:
   353	    if field not in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   354	        raise PlannerError(
   355	            ErrorCode.validation, "field is not an editable item field", {"field": field}
   356	        )
   357	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   358	    prev = getattr(item, field)
   359	    stored: str | None = value
   360	    if field == "priority":
   361	        if value is None:
   362	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid priority", {"value": value})
   363	        try:
   364	            stored = Priority(value).value
   365	        except ValueError as exc:
   366	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid priority", {"value": value}) from exc
   367	    elif field == "project":
   368	        if value is None:
   369	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid project", {"value": value})
   370	        try:
   371	            stored = Project(value).value
   372	        except ValueError as exc:
   373	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid project", {"value": value}) from exc
   374	    now = clock.now_unix()
   375	    with _tx(conn):
   376	        conn.execute(
   377	            f"UPDATE sprint_items SET {field} = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   378	            (stored, now, item_id),
   379	        )
   380	        append_event(
   381	            conn,
   382	            item_id,
   383	            EventKind.item_updated,
   384	            {"field": field, "from": prev, "to": stored},
   385	            now,
   386	        )
   387	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)

   389	
   390	def transition_item_status(
   391	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   392	    item_id: str,
   393	    to_status: ItemStatus,
   394	    *,
   395	    clock: Clock,
   396	    by_agent: bool = True,
   397	    blocked_by: Sequence[str] | None = None,
   398	) -> SprintItem:
   399	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   400	    from_status = item.status
   401	    intended: list[str] = list(blocked_by or []) if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked else []
   402	    verdict = (
   403	        classify_agent_transition(from_status, to_status, intended)
   404	        if by_agent
   405	        else classify_human_transition(from_status, to_status, intended)
   406	    )
   407	    if verdict is TransitionVerdict.forbidden:
   408	        raise PlannerError(
   409	            ErrorCode.item_transition_forbidden,
   410	            "transition not permitted",
   411	            {"from": from_status.value, "to": to_status.value},
   412	        )
   413	    if verdict is TransitionVerdict.missing_blockers:
   414	        raise PlannerError(
   415	            ErrorCode.validation,
   416	            "blocked requires a non-empty blocked_by",
   417	            {"to": "blocked"},
   418	        )
   419	    new_blocked_by = intended if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked else []
   420	    cause = "agent" if by_agent else "human"
   421	    now = clock.now_unix()
   422	    with _tx(conn):
   423	        conn.execute(
   424	            "UPDATE sprint_items SET status = ?, blocked_by = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   425	            (to_status.value, json.dumps(new_blocked_by), now, item_id),
   426	        )
   427	        append_event(
   428	            conn,
   429	            item_id,
   430	            EventKind.item_status_changed,
   431	            {"from": from_status.value, "to": to_status.value, "cause": cause},
   432	            now,
   433	        )
   434	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   435	
   436	
   437	def propose_item_status(
   438	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   439	    item_id: str,
   440	    to_status: ItemStatus,
   441	    *,
   442	    note: str | None,
   443	    proposed_by: str,
   444	    clock: Clock,
   445	) -> SprintItem:
   446	    if to_status not in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
   447	        raise PlannerError(
   448	            ErrorCode.validation,
   449	            "only done/deferred_next_sprint are proposable",
   450	            {"to": to_status.value},
   451	        )
   452	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   453	    if item.status in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
   454	        raise PlannerError(
   455	            ErrorCode.validation,
   456	            "item status is terminal; no further proposals",
   457	            {"status": item.status.value},
   458	        )
   459	    now = clock.now_unix()
   460	    with _tx(conn):
   461	        existing = item.status_proposal
   462	        if existing is not None:
   463	            append_event(
   464	                conn,
   465	                item_id,
   466	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   467	                {
   468	                    "field": "status",
   469	                    "replaced_body": {
   470	                        "to_status": existing.to_status.value,
   471	                        "note": existing.note,
   472	                        "proposed_by": existing.proposed_by,
   473	                        "created_at": existing.created_at,
   474	                    },
   475	                },
   476	                now,
   477	            )
   478	        proposal = ItemStatusProposal(
   479	            to_status=to_status, note=note, proposed_by=proposed_by, created_at=now
   480	        )
   481	        conn.execute(
   482	            "UPDATE sprint_items SET status_proposal = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   483	            (_proposal_to_json(proposal), now, item_id),
   484	        )
   485	        append_event(
   486	            conn,
   487	            item_id,
   488	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   489	            {
   490	                "field": "status",
   491	                "body": {"to_status": to_status.value, "note": note},
   492	                "proposed_by": proposed_by,
   493	            },
   494	            now,
   495	        )
   496	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   497	
   498	
   499	def accept_item_status(
   500	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str, *, clock: Clock, resolved_by: str = "human"
   501	) -> SprintItem:
   502	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   503	    if item.status_proposal is None:
   504	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no pending status proposal", {})
   505	    to_status = item.status_proposal.to_status
   506	    from_status = item.status
   507	    now = clock.now_unix()
   508	    with _tx(conn):
   509	        conn.execute(
   510	            "UPDATE sprint_items SET status = ?, status_proposal = NULL, "
   511	            "blocked_by = '[]', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   512	            (to_status.value, now, item_id),
   513	        )
   514	        append_event(
   515	            conn,
   516	            item_id,
   517	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   518	            {
   519	                "field": "status",
   520	                "body": {"to_status": to_status.value},

Five classic scripts compose under `window.Planner` with zero build tooling: `config.js` (constants), `api.js` (fetchJson with PlannerError envelope passthrough + the invalidation bus: WS `/api/events?since=` client that reads only `msg.cursor`, trailing 250ms debounce from `/api/meta`, exactly one subscriber, reconnect with 500-to-10000ms doubling backoff), `markdown.js` (escape-first safe renderer, single `innerHTML` in the codebase, scheme-whitelisted hrefs hardened against C0-control normalization bypass), `components.js` (the seven D11 primitives, `createElement`/`textContent` only), `app.js` (hash router with exact route shapes, screen registry with overwrite-wins for T15–T17, quiet one-line placeholders, meta-then-WS-then-route boot). `app.css` styles everything exclusively through `var(--token)` — the literal-value grep is empty. `server.py` changed in exactly one place: the `_SHELL` string now serves the real shell (tokens.css, app.css, then the five scripts in dependency order). `smoke.py` (ticket folder, per T10 convention) boots in-process uvicorn in test mode and drives headless chromium through seven checks.

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/core/ws.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§9 event tailer. WS /api/events?since=<id> tails the events table and pushes
     2	{events, cursor} batches — an invalidation signal, non-empty batches only, no
     3	server-side reconciliation. One sqlite connection per WS connection, created and
     4	used on the event-loop thread (sqlite3 same-thread) and closed when the coroutine
     5	ends, including on cancellation.
     6	
     7	A per-connection watcher task drains the receive side: it awaits websocket.receive()
     8	until the ASGI "websocket.disconnect" arrives — sent both when the client closes and
     9	when uvicorn tears the connection down on shutdown. The poll loop wakes on that signal
    10	(instead of sleeping blind), so a parked idle tailer exits promptly on Ctrl-C and never
    11	holds graceful shutdown open, and a silently-gone client is noticed without a send.
    12	
    13	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py family)."""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import asyncio
    18	import sqlite3
    19	from collections.abc import Callable
    20	
    21	from fastapi import WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
    22	
    23	from planner.core.contracts import EventRow, JsonDict
    24	from planner.core.events import read_events_since
    25	
    26	
    27	def _event_json(row: EventRow) -> JsonDict:
    28	    return {
    29	        "id": row.id,
    30	        "entity_id": row.entity_id,
    31	        "kind": row.kind,
    32	        "payload": row.payload,
    33	        "created_at": row.created_at,
    34	    }
    35	
    36	
    37	async def _watch_disconnect(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
    38	    while True:
    39	        message = await websocket.receive()
    40	        if message["type"] == "websocket.disconnect":
    41	            return
    42	
    43	
    44	async def tail_events(
    45	    websocket: WebSocket,
    46	    since: int,
    47	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    48	    ws_poll_ms: int,
    49	    events_read_limit: int,
    50	) -> None:
    51	    await websocket.accept()
    52	    conn = conn_factory()
    53	    watcher = asyncio.create_task(_watch_disconnect(websocket))
    54	    try:
    55	        cursor = since
    56	        while not watcher.done():
    57	            batch = read_events_since(conn, cursor, events_read_limit)
    58	            if batch:
    59	                cursor = batch[-1].id
    60	                await websocket.send_json(
    61	                    {"events": [_event_json(e) for e in batch], "cursor": cursor}
    62	                )
    63	                if len(batch) == events_read_limit:
    64	                    continue
    65	            else:
    66	                await asyncio.wait({watcher}, timeout=ws_poll_ms / 1000)
    67	    except WebSocketDisconnect:
    68	        pass
    69	    finally:
    70	        watcher.cancel()
    71	        try:
    72	            await asyncio.wait({watcher})
    73	        finally:
    74	            conn.close()

     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from datetime import date
     8	from typing import Final
     9	
    10	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import AtCap, FieldName, TicketState
    12	from planner.tickets.logic import machine
    13	
    14	HUMAN_ACTOR: Final[str] = "human"
    15	
    16	
    17	def is_human(actor: str) -> bool:
    18	    return actor == HUMAN_ACTOR
    19	
    20	
    21	def require_human(actor: str, action: str) -> None:
    22	    if actor != HUMAN_ACTOR:
    23	        raise PlannerError(
    24	            ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
    25	            f"{action} is a human-only action",
    26	            {"action": action, "actor": actor},
    27	        )
    28	
    29	
    30	def check_agent_proposal(
    31	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, field: FieldName
    32	) -> None:
    33	    if machine.is_terminal(state):
    34	        raise PlannerError(
    35	            ErrorCode.validation, "no proposals on a terminal ticket", {"state": state.value}
    36	        )
    37	    gating = machine.gating_field(state)
    38	    if not machine.at_or_beyond_ceiling(state, ceiling):
    39	        return
    40	    if at_cap is AtCap.stop:
    41	        raise PlannerError(
    42	            ErrorCode.at_cap_stop,
    43	            "ticket is at its ceiling with at_cap=stop",
    44	            {
    45	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    46	                "state": state.value,
    47	                "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    48	                "at_cap": "stop",
    49	            },
    50	        )
    51	    if field is not gating:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation,
    54	            "at the ceiling agents may propose only the current gating field",
    55	            {
    56	                "field": field.value,
    57	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    58	                "state": state.value,
    59	            },
    60	        )
    61	
    62	
    63	def check_recap_writable(state: TicketState) -> None:
    64	    if state is TicketState.needs_success or state is TicketState.dropped:
    65	        raise PlannerError(
    66	            ErrorCode.recap_too_early,
    67	            "recap is writable only past needs_success",
    68	            {"state": state.value},
    69	        )
    70	
    71	
    72	def check_sprint_assignable(ticket_id: str, sprint_item_id: str | None) -> None:
    73	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
    74	        raise PlannerError(
    75	            ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
    76	            "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
    77	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
    78	        )
    79	
    80	
    81	def validate_title(title: str, max_chars: int) -> None:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "title must be non-empty")
    84	    if len(title) > max_chars:
    85	        raise PlannerError(
    86	            ErrorCode.title_too_long,
    87	            f"title exceeds {max_chars} characters",
    88	            {"length": len(title), "max": max_chars},
    89	        )
    90	
    91	
    92	def validate_deadline(deadline: str | None) -> None:
    93	    if deadline is None:
    94	        return
    95	    try:
    96	        date.fromisoformat(deadline)
    97	    except ValueError as exc:

   100	        ) from exc

   105	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"{what} must be non-empty")
